# Skills · B · part 133

824 entries, Business Model Canvas Coach to business-devis-logiciel. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **Business Model Canvas Coach**: Build a complete 9-block Business Model Canvas with unit economics validation using Brennan Collins' methodology — Right-Side-First sequencing, the 'Standalone Company' viability test, LTV:CAC ratio analysis, and the 'Starving Crowd' customer segment test. Catches feature-builder thinking, vague segments, missing revenue streams, and unvalidated economics. ([BrennanJCollins/UnabatedPM-coaching](https://github.com/BrennanJCollins/UnabatedPM-coaching/tree/HEAD/unabatedpm-business-building/skills/business-model-canvas) · ★5)
- **Business Model Design — Canvas, Patterns & Alternatives**: Design or innovate a business model — map the current or proposed model on the Business Model Canvas and St. Gallen magic triangle, generate at least three genuinely different alternatives from proven patterns (St. Gallen 55, Strategyzer Invincible Company), stress-test each against the environment map, and choose one with the assumptions it rests on. ([pantheonzeus/charness](https://github.com/pantheonzeus/charness/tree/HEAD/resources/skills/business-design/business-model-design))
